## 正本（オラクル）

- 設計: /home/y-saito/Developments/hareruya-design-docs/design_impl_drift_report/design_audit/0506/sheets/<シートID>.txt
  （正本HTML設計書 0506 のシート本文を機械抽出したもの。**設計の正はここだけ**）
  レイアウト図は /home/y-saito/Developments/hareruya-design-docs/design_impl_drift_report/design_audit/0506/images/<シートID>_img*.png

## 探してほしいもの（優先順）

### 1. ハルシネーション（最優先）

引用も実装参照も実在するのに、**主張だけが事実と違う**もの。機械検査では捕まりません。
すでに機械で次は確認済みなので、ここを繰り返す必要はありません。

- 設計根拠_引用が正本シート本文に逐語で実在すること（全件すべて実在）
- 実装参照が ee 内に実在するファイル:行であること（全件すべて実在）

疑ってほしいのは次です。

- 「〜していない」と書いてあるが、実は**別の場所でやっている**
  （親クラス・トレイト・イベントリスナ・サブスクライバ・ミドルウェア・
   フォームの制約・バリデータ・DBの制約・Doctrineのライフサイクル）
- 「実装が無い」と書いてあるが、**探し方が悪くて見つけられていない**だけ
- 実装の分岐の読み違い（条件の向き・early return・例外経路）
- 行番号がずれていて、引用した行が主張と関係ない

### 2. 過剰指摘

次は規約で対象外です。指摘になっていたら挙げてください。

- 実装手段の差（通信方式・認可設定・内部配線・クラス名・呼び出し順・join の種類）
- 応答にも画面にも出ない内部挙動
- 現行仕様に書かれた現行ソースの不具合・不用意な副作用（踏襲対象ではない）。
  ただし機能として定義された挙動（警告表示・並び順・据え置き時間等）は要求として扱う
- 現行仕様とリニューアル後の仕様が食い違う箇所は**後者が優先**。現行仕様側は対象外
- 廃止・フェーズ2と正本が宣言しているもの
- 見出し・表の見出し行・区分行・前置き・運用想定・背景説明
- 設計根拠_引用が**仕様を述べていない**もの（項目名・備考・表のヘッダの読み違い）

### 3. 重複

根本原因キーが違っても、実装実態が同じ欠陥を指していれば重複です。
機械で「判定＋実装実態の完全一致」は確認済みです。**文言が違う重複**を探してください。

### 4. 重要度の過大

P1/P2/P3 が業務影響と迂回可否に見合っているか。

## 指摘対象外（これを指摘しても却下します）

- 設計書の書き方・粒度・文体
- 指摘が**少なすぎる**という主張（過小報告の指摘は別途人が見ます）
- 正本シート本文に無いことを根拠にした「設計にこう書くべき」という主張
- MATCHED・OUT_OF_SCOPE・UNVERIFIABLE と判定したものの妥当性
  （成果物には載っていません。本レビューの範囲外）

## 出力形式

指摘が無ければ `NONE` の1語だけ。

指摘ごとに次を書いてください。

```
要求ID:
判定: ハルシネーション | 過剰指摘 | 重複 | 重要度過大
主張: 成果物が何と言っているか
実際: 実装の file:line を必ず示す。何行目に何と書いてあるか
修正案: 取り下げ / 判定をこう変える / 重要度をこうする / どの要求IDへ畳む
```

**重要度過大と判断したときは、修正案に「P3へ変更する」のように正しい重要度を必ず書いてください。**
書かないと修正が適用されず、重要度は過大なまま残ります。
**重複と判断したときは、修正案に畳む先の要求ID（sheet-N-RNNN）を必ず書いてください。**

---
name: design-impl-drift-audit
description: Compare HTML design specifications against an implementation to find functional drift and produce an evidence-backed HTML report. Use when Codex is asked to treat design docs as the source of truth, audit code such as controllers/services/forms/repositories/templates/batches/CSV definitions, continue a partially completed drift report, or update findings JSON/HTML for gaps between design and implementation.

# Design Impl Drift Audit

## Purpose

Use this skill to audit functional differences between HTML design specifications and an implementation repository, with the design docs treated as authoritative. Produce or continue a report that lists concrete differences with design locations, implementation locations, confidence, severity, and impact.

## Repository Discovery

1. Find the design-doc directory and implementation directory from the user request. If omitted, infer from nearby names such as `*-design-docs` and application repos.
2. Look for an existing report before starting new work. Prefer directories named `design_impl_drift_report`, existing `findings.json`, per-function `findings/*.json`, and `index.html`.
3. Count total target specs and audited specs so the report clearly states scope and remaining work.
4. Preserve existing findings. Append or refine; do not overwrite unrelated prior work.

## Audit Workflow

For each target HTML spec:

1. Identify the feature ID, route/action name, UI template, CSV/batch/API behavior, and acceptance rules from the HTML design spec.
2. Extract a requirement inventory from the HTML spec before looking at code. This inventory must include not only route/screen names, but every concrete requirement in 処理概要, 図形・テキストボックス, 項目表, 表示メッセージ, 業務ルール・計算, 入力項目, エッジケース, データ整合性, API/バッチ結果, 入出力, DB操作, バリデーション, 権限・認可, 画面遷移, エラー処理, ログ・監査, セッション, Cookie, and 排他制御. Treat bullets such as "押下時", "場合", "登録/更新/保存", "遷移", "保持", "表示/非表示", "表示切り替え", "エリアを表示", "誘導", "エラー", "判定", "連携", time limits, thresholds, and retry/idempotency rules as auditable requirements. 秒後/分後, `画面を表示してから`, 自動遷移, タイマー, and store/kiosk/account-specific forced redirects are material requirements even if the route and template already exist.
3. For every extracted requirement, create or mentally maintain a `requirementTrace` row with: `requirementId`, `designRef`, `designRequirement`, `implementationSearchTerms`, `implementationRefs`, `implementationActual`, `traceVerdict` (`MATCHED|DRIFT|NOT_FOUND|UNCERTAIN|PHASE2_EXCLUDED`), and `traceReason`. A feature cannot be marked no-drift until all material requirements have a trace verdict other than `NOT_FOUND` or unresolved `UNCERTAIN`.
4. Search the implementation with `rg` across likely files: controllers, services, repositories, form types, entities, migrations, Twig/templates, commands/batches, route definitions, JavaScript, CSV exporters/importers, and PDF/print services. Search by business terms from the requirement, route names, translation keys, DB columns, state names, constants, statuses, time values, thresholds, and side-effect verbs. For timer or auto-navigation requirements, explicitly search client and server paths for `setTimeout`, `setInterval`, millisecond/second constants such as `60000` and `60 * 1000`, `location.href`, `location.assign`, `location.replace`, redirect routes, and the condition that limits the behavior. For a negative result, also search nearby implemented flows so the report can explain why they do not satisfy the design.
5. Compare behavior and user-facing text, not just names. Check input fields, validation, defaults, search conditions, session persistence, buttons/actions, labels, button text, messages, translation keys/locales, permissions, status transitions, CSV columns, sort/order rules, scheduled timing, timeouts/expiration, thresholds, client-side timers, forced auto-navigation, side effects, idempotency, external integrations, conditional display toggles, alternate UI areas, and error handling.
6. Do not treat "route/controller/template exists" as sufficient. Existence only satisfies the route/screen requirement. It does not satisfy individual rules inside the screen, such as timeout handling, cart retention, validation branches, status transitions, DB writes, logging prohibitions, conditional hide/show behavior, alternative contact/help areas, or external API retry behavior.
7. For conditional display requirements, audit each side of the condition separately. If the HTML says "Aの場合はXを非表示、Yを表示 / A以外はXを表示、Yを非表示", record trace rows for the condition detector, X visibility, Y visibility, and the inverse condition. A nearby notice or route is not enough unless the cited template/JS actually renders the required area under the required condition.
8. For labels, button text, messages, and translation keys, do a dedicated near-match pass. Extract user-visible Japanese text from the HTML item/message tables and compare it with implementation locale values and Twig/Form usage. Treat punctuation, okurigana, missing characters, long-vowel marks, wording changes such as `商品を追加` vs `商品の追加`, and business-term changes such as `返金` vs `送金` as audit targets.
9. A text drift finding is complete only when it cites both the translation/source value and the display path that uses it, such as `messages.ja.yaml` plus the `FormType` label or Twig `|trans` call. If the implementation value exists but no display path is verified, mark it `UNCERTAIN` instead of presenting it as confirmed.
10. Do not stamp all functions as if every standard aspect was fully checked. Separate "standard audit target aspects" from actual evidence. For each finding, list the aspects actually supported by evidence; for no-drift functions, include a requirement trace summary naming the concrete HTML requirements checked and implementation refs searched.
11. Record only actionable drift. If evidence is incomplete, mark the finding `UNCERTAIN` and explain what remains ambiguous.
12. For every drift finding, split the result into implementation-ready fields: `designExpectation`, `implementationActual`, `comparisonRows`, `comparisonSummary`, `mismatchReason`, `impact`, `fixTarget`, `requiredChange`, `implementationRefs`, and, when available, short design/implementation snippets from the cited lines. Do not rely on a generic prose paragraph alone.
13. Each `comparisonRows` entry must answer all four questions explicitly: comparison item, exact design value/requirement, exact implementation value/behavior, and why those two do not match. If any of those four cells would be vague, continue investigating before recording the finding.
14. Treat phase labels such as Ph2 as auxiliary context. They can be stored, but the main report must not use phase status as a substitute for concrete behavioral differences and exact implementation locations.
15. If the HTML design spec explicitly says the whole function or scoped subfeature is `フェーズ2で実装する`, `Ph2で対応`, or `Ph1では実装しない`, do not report absence of that planned Phase 2 work as drift or as a clear implementation gap. You may keep a secondary audit note that non-implementation was confirmed, but exclude it from finding counts and implementation-gap counts. Only keep a finding when there is a concrete non-phase mismatch, such as an implemented Phase 1 path behaving differently from the design or user-facing text differing.
16. When no drift is found, add the function to the audited set with an empty findings list only after the requirement trace gate passes. If material HTML requirements are untraced, do not mark `AUDITED_NO_DRIFT_FOUND`; mark the function as requiring review or add `UNCERTAIN` findings with the unresolved requirements.
17. Do not let one confirmed finding hide other requirements in the same function. A function with `AUDITED_WITH_FINDINGS` can still have untraced material requirements; keep those requirements visible in the trace gate until each one is converted to a confirmed finding, an uncertain finding, a matched trace row, or a documented phase/design non-implementation exclusion.
18. For explicitly registered critical requirements, the harness should carry a missing-finding template or an equivalent escalation rule. If the trace is not covered by an existing finding, promote it into findings automatically or fail the quality gate loudly. Do not rely on a hand-maintained `ADDITIONAL_FINDINGS` list as the only path for user-reported or high-risk missing behavior such as timeouts, cart retention, one-minute/30-minute limits, store-account auto redirects, conditional UI areas, status transitions, external calls, or destructive side effects.
19. The HTML report must not expose broad source-signal candidates as if they were implementation gaps. A requirement-level list in the main report must be limited to confirmed or explicitly uncertain actionable drift derived from findings, such as `NOT_FOUND` or `DRIFT` requirement traces and concrete comparison rows. If a source-signal scan is used for triage, keep it out of the main HTML report unless each row has been verified against implementation source and written as a concrete finding.
20. Do not hide time-based or automatic-navigation requirements merely because they are not confirmed findings yet. Requirements involving `画面を表示してから`, seconds/minutes after display, auto redirect/navigation, or timeout-to-cart/top behavior should appear in a separate focused untraced-requirement audit queue when they remain uncovered. This queue is not a confirmed defect count; it exists so these high-risk requirements are reviewed against Controller/Twig/JavaScript/Service source instead of being buried among broad requirement-trace failures.

## Function-Level Implementation Gap Audit

For every HTML spec sheet, maintain a function-level implementation audit even when no drift finding is confirmed yet.

- Extract implementation search terms from the HTML design: route names, URL paths, translation keys, class/controller/service names, batch/API/CSV names, and high-signal business terms from the sheet title and requirement text.
- Search implementation source by function across Controller, Form, Service, Repository, Entity, Migration, Twig, Command/Batch, CSV, PDF/print, API/webhook, locale/messages, JavaScript, and configuration files.
- Store a `functionImplementationAudit` object with `verdict`, `designDerivedTerms`, `candidateRefs`, `candidateCount`, `strongSignalCount`, `searchedAreas`, and `auditNote`.
- Use separate verdicts:
  - `CONFIRMED_IMPLEMENTATION_GAP`: a concrete drift finding already proves missing implementation.
  - `NO_IMPLEMENTATION_ENTRY_FOUND`: no entry candidate was found from design-derived terms; treat as an additional-review candidate, not an automatic confirmed defect.
  - `IMPLEMENTATION_ENTRY_FOUND`: strong source evidence such as route, class, translation key, or path was found.
  - `IMPLEMENTATION_CANDIDATE_FOUND`: weak business-term candidates were found but the entry point is not proven.
  - `PHASE2_NONIMPLEMENTATION_EXPECTED`: HTML design explicitly scopes non-implementation to Phase 2/Ph1 exclusion.
  - `DESIGN_NONIMPLEMENTATION_EXPECTED`: HTML design explicitly says the function or scoped behavior is not implemented by code, such as `実装しない`, `実装不要`, or infrastructure/external-service configuration replacing application code.
- Use `DESIGN_NONIMPLEMENTATION_EXPECTED` as the function-level verdict only when the non-implementation statement applies to the whole function or the implementation entry itself, for example an external WordPress server, Docker image cron, Aurora parameter group, or a bare whole-sheet `実装しない`. If the text only excludes a scoped subfeature, UI alert, HTML hint, logging detail, partial Phase 2 item, or internal job, keep the function's source-entry verdict and mention the scoped exclusion only in the trace/audit note.
- The report must show both confirmed implementation-gap findings and function-level no-entry candidates. Do not merge these counts: implementers need confirmed defects for fixing and no-entry candidates for review planning.
- A function-level candidate is not enough to mark the detailed requirements as implemented. Requirement trace still controls no-drift status.
- Do not set report metadata such as `auditComplete` or narrative notes to "complete" merely because every sheet has a function-level implementation audit. If any material requirement trace remains unresolved, state that all sheets have function-entry coverage but requirement-level audit is incomplete.
- For infrastructure-oriented sheets such as Step Functions, Lambda, WAF/S3 jobs, cron configured outside the application, or managed database settings, distinguish "not found in the requested implementation repository" from "not implemented anywhere". A confirmed implementation-gap finding may be scoped to the audited repository, but the report must say when external infrastructure repositories or cloud configuration were not checked.

## Requirement Trace Gate

Before marking a function no-drift, verify the following:

- Every material HTML requirement has a trace row. Material means it changes behavior, data, display, validation, permission, transition, side effect, external communication, schedule, retry/idempotency, logging/audit, CSV/API/PDF/batch I/O, or user-visible text.
- Each trace row includes exact design text or a tight paraphrase plus line/anchor reference, not only the sheet title.
- Each matched row cites the implementation path and line(s) that actually execute or render the behavior. A nearby class, route, or template is not enough unless the cited code contains the relevant condition/value/side effect.
- Each missing row is converted to a confirmed or uncertain finding. Use confirmed only after searching the direct path and nearby alternative implementations.
- Missing rows remain failures even if the function already has other findings. The gate is requirement-scoped, not function-scoped.
- If an explicit critical requirement has a configured missing-finding template, regenerate the report from the harness and verify the promoted finding appears in both JSON and HTML with design value, implementation behavior, mismatch reason, fix target, and search evidence.
- If a missing row does not yet have enough evidence for a confirmed or uncertain actionable finding, do not count it as an implementation gap and do not put it in the main HTML fix list. Keep the function in an incomplete requirement-trace state, and promote the row only after implementation source has been checked enough to state the exact design requirement, exact implementation behavior, and mismatch reason.
- A generic `auditEvidence` string is not a substitute for requirement traces.
- Negative searches must list the searched terms/areas and why nearby hits do not satisfy the design.

## Finding Format

Store findings in a machine-readable JSON structure. Use this shape for each finding:

```json
{
  "id": "stable-feature-id-001",
  "functionId": "m04-01_admin_stock_stock_search_list",
  "functionName": "在庫一覧",
  "severity": "high|med|low",
  "dimension": "①機能/ルート未実装",
  "confidence": "CONFIRMED|UNCERTAIN",
  "design": {
    "file": "path/to/spec.html",
    "lines": "215-220",
    "summary": "設計書に書かれている仕様"
  },
  "implementation": {
    "file": "path/to/source.php or 不在",
    "lines": "337-344",
    "summary": "実装側の状態"
  },
  "difference": "設計と実装の具体的な差分",
  "comparisonRows": [
    {
      "item": "比較項目名",
      "design": "設計書の値・要求。列名、HTTPメソッド、パス、保存先、表示文言、条件、状態遷移などを具体値で書く",
      "implementation": "実装の値・挙動。実際の列名、メソッド、パス、保存先、文言、条件、処理順を具体値で書く",
      "mismatch": "なぜ一致しないのか。実装者が直すべき差分を一文で書く"
    }
  ],
  "requirementTrace": [
    {
      "requirementId": "stable-requirement-id",
      "designRef": "path/to/spec.html:123",
      "designRequirement": "HTML設計書の個別要求",
      "implementationSearchTerms": ["検索語", "route_name", "methodName"],
      "implementationRefs": ["src/...php:123"],
      "implementationActual": "実装で確認できた挙動、または不在判定",
      "traceVerdict": "MATCHED|DRIFT|NOT_FOUND|UNCERTAIN|PHASE2_EXCLUDED",
      "traceReason": "なぜその判定か"
    }
  ],
  "comparisonSummary": "設計はX、実装はY、したがってZが違う",
  "mismatchReason": "乖離と判断した具体的な理由",
  "impact": "ユーザー・業務・データへの影響",
  "fixTarget": "修正対象のファイル/ルート/サービス/不在箇所",
  "requiredChange": "設計に合わせる場合に必要な修正",
  "implementationRefs": ["src/...php:123"],
  "implementationSnippets": ["123: 実装コードの短い断片"],
  "evidence": ["短い根拠。ファイル名と行番号を含める"]
}
```

The HTML report should make these fields visible as separate blocks so an implementer can answer: what exact value does the design require, what exact value does the code implement, why is that a mismatch, exactly where is the code, and what should be changed.

Use stable IDs and Japanese text when the existing report is Japanese. Keep dimensions consistent with existing findings when continuing a report.

## Severity Guidance

- `high`: missing feature/route, wrong critical business rule, destructive or externally visible behavior, missing required import/export/batch/PDF flow.
- `med`: partial implementation, wrong filtering/sorting/session behavior, missing non-critical columns or validation, incompatible screen behavior.
- `low`: copy/display/default-state differences, ambiguous design wording, minor workflow variance.

## Report Generation

If the project already has a report generator, use it. Otherwise copy or adapt `scripts/build_report.py` from this skill into the report directory, then run it from the repository root or pass paths as needed.

The HTML report must include:

- summary counts: total findings, severities, audited functions, functions with drift
- scope and remaining work
- per-function sections
- each finding with severity, confidence, dimension, audit aspects including label/message/translation-key differences when applicable, design reference, implementation reference, design expectation, implementation actual behavior, `comparisonRows`, mismatch reason, impact, fix target, required change, and evidence
- a comparison table with columns: 比較項目 / 設計書の値・要求 / 実装の値・挙動 / 乖離理由
- links or file paths precise enough for a developer to inspect
- short cited design and implementation snippets when available
- phase/Ph2 data only as hidden or secondary metadata, never as a report column or primary summary
- a function-level implementation audit section showing verdict counts, no-entry candidates, candidate source references, and the search terms that produced them
- a requirement-level difference list derived only from confirmed/uncertain findings, showing the exact design requirement, implementation behavior, mismatch reason, implementation refs, and a link back to the finding. Do not include broad unverified source-signal queues in the main HTML report.
- a focused untraced-requirement section for small, high-risk classes such as display-time timers, automatic redirects, and timeout navigation. Keep it separate from confirmed difference and implementation-gap counts, and include search terms/candidate refs so the next audit step is concrete.

After editing findings, validate JSON and regenerate HTML:

```bash
python3 -m json.tool path/to/design_impl_drift_report/findings.json
python3 path/to/design_impl_drift_report/build_report.py
```

## Quality Bar

- Treat the HTML design spec as source of truth even when implementation looks intentional.
- Cite exact file paths and line numbers whenever available.
- Distinguish `不在` from “not found yet”; use `不在` only after searching likely implementation areas.
- Avoid broad claims such as “not implemented” without search evidence or contrasting nearby implemented features.
- Do not classify Phase 2 planned non-implementation as a defect. A missing route, CSV, batch, API, table, or screen is only a drift finding when the design expects it in the audited implementation phase.
- For label/message drift, prefer exact line evidence and a verified render path over fuzzy string similarity. Fuzzy or near-match scans are candidate generators; they are not findings until source and display usage are confirmed.
- When an external reviewer or another agent reports candidates, verify each candidate against the HTML design and implementation source before adding it. Keep rejected or ambiguous candidates out of confirmed counts, or mark them `UNCERTAIN` with the unresolved reason.
- For absent implementations, include the searched terms/areas, nearby implementation files with line numbers, and a concrete reason those nearby files do not satisfy the design. Do not leave `implRef` as only `不在` in the final report.
- Reject vague findings. A finding is not complete until it names the exact design value and exact implementation value that differ.
- Keep the report cumulative and resumable.

  • 重複・重要度を横断比較して指定形式で結論を出す

sheet-4-R056	店頭買取情報取得	現行仕様	実装違い	P3	IO	applyDate はISO8601形式の日時文字列（例 2024-09-20T10:47:29+09:00）で返す。	applyDate を 'Y/m/d H:i:s' 形式（例 2024/09/20 10:47:29）で返している（src/Eccube/Controller/App/MTGBuyer/V1/Admin/OtcBuyOrderController.php:97）。テストも同じ書式を期待値にしている（tests/Eccube/Tests/Web/App/MTGBuyer/V1/Admin/OtcBuyOrderControllerTest.php:124）。	ISO8601は日付と時刻をハイフン区切り・T連結で表す書式で、スラッシュ区切り・空白連結の値はこれに当たらない。同APIを読むMTGBuyerが申込み時間を解釈する値であり、書式差は日時の解釈失敗や誤表示につながる。同じ節の他のふるまい（freeComment・memberName・qualifiedInvoiceIssuerCode のnull）は一致しているため、この行だけが外れている。

sheet-4-R062	店頭買取情報取得	現行仕様	実装違い	P3	IO	住所は、国が日本のときは都道府県名・住所1・住所2、それ以外の国のときは国名・住所2・住所1 の順に、半角空白区切りで連結した1つの文字列で返す（住所3は連結しない）。	日本のときは 都道府県名・住所1・住所2・住所3、日本以外のときは 国名・住所3・住所2・住所1 と、設計に無い住所3を加えて連結している（src/Eccube/Controller/App/MTGBuyer/V1/Admin/OtcBuyOrderController.php:90-92）。住所3が設定された受注では住所文字列に1区画多く現れる。テストも住所3を含む期待値になっている（tests/Eccube/Tests/Web/App/MTGBuyer/V1/Admin/OtcBuyOrderControllerTest.php:143-149）。	連結する要素と順序が明記されているのに、実装は住所3を追加している。住所3は受注に存在する任意項目（src/Eccube/Entity/DtbOtcBuyOrder.php:76-77）で、値が入っていれば必ず出力に現れるため、MTGBuyerの氏名・住所照合欄に表示される文字列が設計と変わる。空のときは空要素が落ちるので一致する。
sheet-5-R076	店頭買取情報更新	現行仕様	実装違い	P2	ふるまい	適格請求書発行事業者に該当する受注のときだけ、適格請求書発行事業者確認フラグが指定された値（true でも false でも）に更新され、該当しない受注では指定されても更新されない。	受注が適格請求書発行事業者かどうかを見ず、指定値が true のときだけフラグを true にしている。そのため（1）適格請求書発行事業者の受注で false を指定しても確認済みが解除されず、（2）適格請求書発行事業者でない受注に true を指定すると確認済みになる。査定担当者・更新日時の設定は src/Eccube/Service/EntityManager/OtcBuyOrderEntityManager.php:48-49 で一致。	現行実装（pf-api の店頭買取受注詳細更新）は受注側の適格請求書発行事業者フラグを条件に確認済みフラグを指定値へ更新していた。ee は条件が指定値そのものに置き換わっており、受注側の該当有無を参照する箇所が無い。

        private readonly UpdateOtcBuyOrderAction $updateOtcBuyOrderAction,
        private readonly MemberRepository $memberRepository,
        private readonly UpdateIdentificationAction $updateIdentificationAction,
        private readonly MtbIdentificationRepository $mtbIdentificationRepository,
    ) {
    }

    /**
     * 審査未完了の店頭買取受注一覧取得
     */
    #[Route('/%eccube_api_v1_route%/admin/otcBuyOrders.json', name: 'api_admin_otc_buy_orders', methods: ['GET'])]
    public function getUncompletedOtcBuyOrders(): JsonResponse
    {
        $Member = $this->getUser();
        if (!$Member instanceof Member) {
            throw new UnauthenticatedException('認証エラー');
        }

        $shopId = $Member->getBaseInfo()->getId();

        $otcBuyOrders = $this->dtbOtcBuyOrderRepository->getOtcBuyOrdersByShopIdAndStatus($shopId, MtbOtcBuyOrderStatus::ASSESSMENT_UNCOMPLETED_STATUSES);
        $response = array_map(function (array $otcBuyOrder): array {
            $isJapan = $otcBuyOrder['countryId'] === Country::JAPAN;

            $join = static function (array $parts): string {
                $parts = array_map(static fn ($v) => trim((string) ($v ?? '')), $parts);
                $parts = array_values(array_filter($parts, static fn ($v) => $v !== ''));

                return implode(' ', $parts);
            };

            $address = $isJapan ?
                $join([$otcBuyOrder['prefName'] ?? '', $otcBuyOrder['addr01'], $otcBuyOrder['addr02'], $otcBuyOrder['addr03'] ?? '']) :
                $join([$otcBuyOrder['countryName'] ?? '', $otcBuyOrder['addr03'] ?? '', $otcBuyOrder['addr02'], $otcBuyOrder['addr01']]);

            return [
                'otcBuyOrderId' => $otcBuyOrder['id'],
                'assessmentId' => $otcBuyOrder['assessmentId'],
                'applyDate' => $otcBuyOrder['applyDate']?->format('Y/m/d H:i:s') ?? null,
                'freeComment' => $otcBuyOrder['freeComment'] ?? null,
                'memberName' => $otcBuyOrder['memberName'] ?? null,
                'otcOrderStatusId' => $otcBuyOrder['otcOrderStatusId'],
                'returnSupply' => $otcBuyOrder['returnSupply'] ?? null,
                'callFlg' => $otcBuyOrder['callFlg'],
                'adultFlg' => $otcBuyOrder['adultFlg'],
                'playingFlg' => $otcBuyOrder['playingFlg'],
                'orderStatusName' => $otcBuyOrder['otcOrderStatusName'],
                'identificationId' => $otcBuyOrder['identificationId'] ?? null,
                'qualifiedInvoiceIssuerFlg' => $otcBuyOrder['qualifiedInvoiceIssuerFlg'],
                'qualifiedInvoiceIssuerConfirmationFlg' => $otcBuyOrder['qualifiedInvoiceIssuerConfirmationFlg'],
                'qualifiedInvoiceIssuerCode' => $otcBuyOrder['qualifiedInvoiceIssuerCode'] ?? null,
                'customerInfo' => [
                    'firstName' => $otcBuyOrder['firstName'],
                    'lastName' => $otcBuyOrder['lastName'],
                    'birth' => $otcBuyOrder['birth']->format('Y-m-d'),
                    'telNo' => $otcBuyOrder['telNo'],
                    'zipcode' => $otcBuyOrder['zipcode'],
                    'jobName' => $otcBuyOrder['jobName'] ?? null,
                    'address' => $address,
                ],
            ];
        }, $otcBuyOrders);

        return new JsonResponse($response);
    }

    /**
     * 店頭買取情報更新API
     * このAPIは、他のAPIとは異なりJSON形式のリクエストが前提となる
     */
    #[Route('/%eccube_api_v1_route%/admin/otcBuyOrder/{id}.json', name: 'api_admin_otc_buy_order_update', methods: ['PUT'])]
    public function updateOtcBuyOrder(int $id, #[MapRequestPayload] UpdateOtcBuyOrderDto $updateOtcBuyOrderDto): JsonResponse
    {
        $OtcBuyOrder = $this->dtbOtcBuyOrderRepository->find($id);
        if (!$OtcBuyOrder instanceof DtbOtcBuyOrder) {
            throw new NotFoundException('買取情報が見つかりません');
        }

        $Member = $this->getUser();
        if (!$Member instanceof Member) {
            throw new UnauthenticatedException('認証エラー');
        }

        try {
            $this->updateOtcBuyOrderAction->handle(new UpdateOtcBuyOrderActionInput($OtcBuyOrder, $Member, $updateOtcBuyOrderDto));
        } catch (BaseApiException $e) {
            throw $e;
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            throw new InternalException('システムエラーが発生しました', $e);
        }

        return new JsonResponse(['code' => Response::HTTP_OK], Response::HTTP_OK);
    }

    /**
     * フリーコメントの更新
     */
    #[Route('/%eccube_api_v1_route%/admin/otcBuyOrder/{id}/freeComment.json', name: 'api_admin_otc_buy_order_update_free_comment', methods: ['PUT'])]
    public function updateFreeComment(int $id, Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $OtcBuyOrder = $this->dtbOtcBuyOrderRepository->find($id);
        if (!$OtcBuyOrder instanceof DtbOtcBuyOrder) {
            throw new NotFoundException('買取情報が見つかりません');
        }

        $raw = $request->request->get('free_comment');
        if ($raw === null) {
            throw new MissingRequiredParameterException('コメントを入力してください');
        }

        $freeComment = (string) $raw;

        $Member = $this->getUser();
        if (!$Member instanceof Member) {
            throw new UnauthenticatedException('認証エラー');
        }

        try {
            $this->updateFreeCommentAction->handle(new UpdateFreeCommentInput($OtcBuyOrder, $Member, $freeComment));

            // システムエラーの場合は隠蔽して500エラーを返す
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            throw new InternalException('システムエラーが発生しました', $e);
        }

        return new JsonResponse(['code' => Response::HTTP_OK], Response::HTTP_OK);
    }

    /**
     * ステータスの更新
     */
    #[Route('/%eccube_api_v1_route%/admin/otcBuyOrder/{id}/status.json', name: 'api_admin_otc_buy_order_update_status', methods: ['PUT'])]
    public function updateStatus(int $id, Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $OtcBuyOrder = $this->dtbOtcBuyOrderRepository->find($id);
        if (!$OtcBuyOrder instanceof DtbOtcBuyOrder) {
            throw new NotFoundException('買取情報が見つかりません');
        }

        $raw = $request->request->get('status');
        if ($raw === null || $raw === '' || !ctype_digit((string) $raw)) {
            throw new MissingRequiredParameterException('ステータスが不正です');
        }
        $statusId = (int) $raw;

        $Status = $this->entityManager->find(MtbOtcBuyOrderStatus::class, $statusId);
        if (!$Status instanceof MtbOtcBuyOrderStatus) {
            throw new InvalidParameterException('ステータスが見つかりません');
        }

        $Member = $this->getUser();
        if (!$Member instanceof Member) {
            throw new UnauthenticatedException('認証エラー');
        }

        try {
            $this->updateStatusAction->handle(new UpdateStatusInput($OtcBuyOrder, $Member, $Status));

            // カスタムエラーの場合はそのまま返す
        } catch (BaseApiException $e) {
            throw $e;
            // システムエラーの場合は隠蔽して500エラーを返す
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            throw new InternalException('システムエラーが発生しました', $e);
        }

        return new JsonResponse(['code' => Response::HTTP_OK], Response::HTTP_OK);
    }

    /**
     * ダブルチェック者の更新
     */
    #[Route('/%eccube_api_v1_route%/admin/otcBuyOrder/{id}/doubleCheckMember.json', name: 'api_admin_otc_buy_order_update_double_check_member', methods: ['PUT'])]
    public function updateDoubleCheckMember(int $id, Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $OtcBuyOrder = $this->dtbOtcBuyOrderRepository->find($id);
        if (!$OtcBuyOrder instanceof DtbOtcBuyOrder) {
            throw new NotFoundException('買取情報が見つかりません');
        }

        $raw = $request->request->get('double_check_member_id');
        if ($raw === null) {
            throw new MissingRequiredParameterException('ダブルチェック者を入力してください');
        }

        $Member = $this->getUser();
        if (!$Member instanceof Member) {
            throw new UnauthenticatedException('認証エラー');
        }

        $doubleCheckMemberId = (int) $raw;

        $DoubleCheckMember = $this->memberRepository->find($doubleCheckMemberId);
        if (!$DoubleCheckMember instanceof Member) {
            throw new NotFoundException('ダブルチェック者が見つかりません');
        }

        try {
            $this->updateDoubleCheckMemberAction->handle(new UpdateDoubleCheckMemberInput($OtcBuyOrder, $Member, $DoubleCheckMember));

            // カスタムエラーの場合はそのまま返す
        } catch (BaseApiException $e) {
            throw $e;
            // システムエラーの場合は隠蔽して500エラーを返す
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            throw new InternalException('システムエラーが発生しました', $e);
        }

        return new JsonResponse(['code' => Response::HTTP_OK], Response::HTTP_OK);
    }

    /**
     * 身分証明書種別の更新
     */
    #[Route('/%eccube_api_v1_route%/admin/otcBuyOrder/{id}/identification.json', name: 'api_admin_otc_buy_order_update_identification', methods: ['PUT'])]
    public function updateIdentification(int $id, Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $OtcBuyOrder = $this->dtbOtcBuyOrderRepository->find($id);
        if (!$OtcBuyOrder instanceof DtbOtcBuyOrder) {
            throw new NotFoundException('買取情報が見つかりません');
        }

        $raw = $request->request->get('identification');
        if ($raw === null) {
            throw new MissingRequiredParameterException('正しい証明書IDを入力してください');
        }

        $identificationId = (int) $raw;

        $Identification = $this->mtbIdentificationRepository->find($identificationId);
        if (!$Identification instanceof MtbIdentification) {
            throw new NotFoundException('正しい証明書IDを入力してください');
        }

        $Member = $this->getUser();
        if (!$Member instanceof Member) {
            throw new UnauthenticatedException('認証エラー');
        }

        try {
            $this->updateIdentificationAction->handle(new UpdateIdentificationInput($OtcBuyOrder, $Member, $Identification));

            // カスタムエラーの場合はそのまま返す
        } catch (BaseApiException $e) {
            throw $e;
            // システムエラーの場合は隠蔽して500エラーを返す
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            throw new InternalException('システムエラーが発生しました', $e);
        }

        return new JsonResponse(['code' => Response::HTTP_OK], Response::HTTP_OK);
    }
}

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Eccube\Repository\Master;

use Doctrine\DBAL\ArrayParameterType;
use Doctrine\ORM\Query\ResultSetMapping;
use Doctrine\ORM\QueryBuilder;
use Doctrine\Persistence\ManagerRegistry;
use Eccube\Dto\Repository\Master\GetBuyingCardsQueryResponseDto;
use Eccube\Entity\Master\MtbCard;
use Eccube\Entity\Master\MtbCardCondition;
use Eccube\Entity\Master\MtbCardset;
use Eccube\Entity\Master\ProductStatus;
use Eccube\Entity\Product;
use Eccube\Repository\AbstractRepository;
use Eccube\Service\RedisService;
use Eccube\Util\StringUtil;

/**
 * @extends AbstractRepository<MtbCard>
 */
class MtbCardRepository extends AbstractRepository
{
    private const LANG_JA = 1;
    private const LANG_EN = 2;

    public const DEFAULT_SORT_KEY = 'id';
    public const DEFAULT_SORT_ORDER = 'DESC';

    /**
     * @var array<string, string>
     */
    private array $sortKeys = [
        'id' => 'c.id',
        'cmc' => 'c.cmc',
        'name_jp' => 'c.name_jp',
        'name_en' => 'c.name_en',
    ];

    private const CARD_SEARCH_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS = 600;

    public function __construct(
        ManagerRegistry $registry,
        private readonly RedisService $redisService,
    ) {
        parent::__construct($registry, MtbCard::class);
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, string>
     */
    public function getSortKeys(): array
    {
        return $this->sortKeys;
    }

    public function get(int $id = 1): ?MtbCard
    {
        return $this->find($id);
    }

    /**
     * CardDetailIDリストから買取カード情報を取得
     *
     * @param array<int, int> $cardDetailIds カード詳細IDの配列
     * @param int $storeId 店舗ID
     * @param int $stockLocationId 在庫場所ID
     *
     * @return array<int, GetBuyingCardsQueryResponseDto>
     */
    public function getBuyingCardsByCardDetailIds(
        array $cardDetailIds,
        int $storeId,
        int $stockLocationId,
    ): array {
        $result = $this->buildBuyingCardsQuery($storeId, $stockLocationId)
            ->andWhere('cardDetail.id IN (:cardDetailIds)')
            ->setParameter('cardDetailIds', $cardDetailIds)
            ->getQuery()
            ->getResult();

        return GetBuyingCardsQueryResponseDto::fromDqlResult($result);
    }

    /**
     * 商品IDリストから買取カード情報を取得
     *
     * @param array<int, int> $productIds 商品IDの配列
     * @param int $storeId 店舗ID
     * @param int $stockLocationId 在庫場所ID
     *
     * @return array<int, GetBuyingCardsQueryResponseDto>
     */
    public function getBuyingCardsByProductIds(
        array $productIds,
        int $storeId,
        int $stockLocationId,
    ): array {
        $result = $this->buildBuyingCardsQuery($storeId, $stockLocationId)
            ->andWhere('product.id IN (:productIds)')
            ->setParameter('productIds', $productIds)
            ->getQuery()
            ->getResult();

        return GetBuyingCardsQueryResponseDto::fromDqlResult($result);
    }

    /**
     * カード名から買取カード情報を前方一致で取得
     *
     * @param string $cardName カード名
     * @param int $storeId 店舗ID
     * @param int $stockLocationId 在庫場所ID
     *
     * @return array<int, GetBuyingCardsQueryResponseDto>
     */
    public function getBuyingCardsByCardName(
        string $cardName,
        int $storeId,
        int $stockLocationId,
    ): array {
        $result = $this->buildBuyingCardsQuery($storeId, $stockLocationId)
            ->andWhere('product.name LIKE :cardName')
            ->setParameter('cardName', $cardName.'%')
            ->getQuery()
            ->getResult();

        return GetBuyingCardsQueryResponseDto::fromDqlResult($result);
    }

    /**
     * 買取カード情報取得用の共通クエリビルダー
     * Product, ProductClassが公開・非公開（廃止以外）のもののみを取得する
     * 高額カードは除外する
     *
     * @param int $storeId 店舗ID
     * @param int $stockLocationId 在庫場所ID
     */
    private function buildBuyingCardsQuery(int $storeId, int $stockLocationId): QueryBuilder
    {
        return $this->createQueryBuilder('card')
            ->join('card.CardDetails', 'cardDetail')
            ->join('cardDetail.Products', 'product')
            ->join('product.ProductClasses', 'productClass')
            ->join('productClass.ProductStock', 'productStock')  // ProductClassからProductStockを取得
            ->leftJoin('product.ProductImage', 'productImage', 'WITH', 'productImage.sort_no = 0')  // 最初の商品画像
            ->join('productClass.CardCondition', 'cardCondition')
            ->leftJoin('product.StorageCode', 'storageCode')
            ->leftJoin('cardDetail.Cardset', 'cardset')
            ->leftJoin('cardDetail.Promotion', 'promotion')
            ->join('productClass.Language', 'language')
            ->join('cardDetail.Rarity', 'rarity')
            ->leftJoin('productClass.Section', 'section')
            ->andWhere('IDENTITY(product.Status) IN (:productStatusIds)')
            ->andWhere('IDENTITY(productClass.Status) IN (:productStatusIds)')
            ->andWhere('productStock.baseInfo = :storeId')
            ->andWhere('productStock.stockLocationId = :stockLocationId')
            ->andWhere('productClass.high_price_code IS NULL') // 高額カード除外
            ->setParameter('productStatusIds', [ProductStatus::DISPLAY_SHOW, ProductStatus::DISPLAY_HIDE]) // 移植元の論理削除＝廃止とみなして、公開・非公開を抽出
            ->setParameter('storeId', $storeId)
            ->setParameter('stockLocationId', $stockLocationId)
            ->select('
                card.id AS cardId,
                card.name_jp AS cardNameJp,
                card.name_en AS cardNameEn,
                cardDetail.id AS detailId,
                cardset.code AS cardSetCode,
                cardset.name_jp AS cardSetNameJp,
                cardDetail.foil_flg AS foilFlg,
                cardDetail.card_no AS cardNo,
                promotion.name_jp AS promotionNameJp,
                product.id AS productId,
                product.name AS productNameJp,
                product.name_en AS productNameEn,
                rarity.code AS rarityCode,
                storageCode.name AS storageCodeName,
                language.code AS languageCode,
                cardCondition.code AS conditionCode,
                productClass.id AS productClassId,
                productClass.code AS productClassCode,
                productClass.buy_price AS buyPrice,
                productClass.standardPrice AS standardPrice,
                productStock.stock AS stock,
                section.id AS sectionId,
                productImage.file_name AS imageFileName
            ');
    }

    /**
     * 指定したカードセットに州力されたカードを取得用のクエリビルダー
     */
    public function getQueryBuilderByCardset(MtbCardset $Cardset): QueryBuilder
    {
        return $this->createQueryBuilder('card')
            ->join('card.CardDetails', 'cardDetail')
            ->andWhere('cardDetail.Cardset = :Cardset')
            ->setParameter('Cardset', $Cardset)
            ->orderBy('card.id', 'ASC');
    }

    /**
     * 検索条件からQueryBuilderを取得
     *
     * @param array<string, mixed> $searchData 検索条件の配列
     */
    public function getQueryBuilderBySearchData(array $searchData): QueryBuilder
    {
        $qb = $this->createQueryBuilder('c')
            ->select('c');

        // キーワード検索 (multi)
        if (isset($searchData['multi']) && StringUtil::isNotBlank($searchData['multi'])) {
            $multi = $searchData['multi'];
            $qb->andWhere('c.name_jp LIKE :multi OR c.name_en LIKE :multi OR c.text_jp LIKE :multi OR c.text_en LIKE :multi')
                ->setParameter('multi', '%'.$multi.'%');
        }

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

/*
 * This file is part of EC-CUBE
 *
 * Copyright(c) EC-CUBE CO.,LTD. All Rights Reserved.
 *
 * http://www.ec-cube.co.jp/

namespace Eccube\Service\App\MTGBuyer\V1;

use Eccube\Dto\Repository\Master\GetBuyingCardsQueryResponseDto;

class BuyingCardsFormatter
{
    /**
     * フラットな配列を階層構造の連想配列に変換
     *
     * @param array<int, GetBuyingCardsQueryResponseDto> $dtoData
     *
     * @return array{cards: array<int, array{
     *     cardNameJp: string,
     *     cardNameEn: string|null,
     *     imageFileName: string|null,
     *     details: array<int, array{
     *         cardsetCode: string|null,
     *         cardsetName: string|null,
     *         foilFlg: int,
     *         cardNo: string|null,
     *         promotionName: string|null,
     *         productId: int,
     *         productNameJp: string,
     *         productNameEn: string|null,
     *         rarityCode: string|null,
     *         storageCodeName: string|null,
     *         languageClasses: array<string, array{
     *             conditionClasses: array<string, array{
     *                 productClassId: int,
     *                 productClassCode: string,
     *                 buyPrice: int|null,
     *                 price: int|null,
     *                 stock: int,
     *                 sectionId: int|null
     *             }>
     *         }>
     *     }>
     * }>|\stdClass}
     */
    public function format(array $dtoData): array
    {
        $cards = [];

        foreach ($dtoData as $dto) {
            if ($dto->languageCode === null) {
                continue;
            }

            if ($dto->conditionCode === null) {
                continue;
            }

            $cardId = $dto->cardId;
            $detailId = $dto->detailId;
            $languageCode = $dto->languageCode;
            $conditionCode = $dto->conditionCode;

            // Cardレベル
            if (!isset($cards[$cardId])) {
                $cards[$cardId] = [
                    'cardNameJp' => $dto->cardNameJp,
                    'cardNameEn' => $dto->cardNameEn,
                    'imageFileName' => $dto->imageFileName,
                    'details' => [],
                ];
            }

            // Detailレベル
            if (!isset($cards[$cardId]['details'][$detailId])) {
                $cards[$cardId]['details'][$detailId] = [
                    'cardsetCode' => $dto->cardSetCode,
                    'cardsetName' => $dto->cardSetNameJp,
                    'foilFlg' => $dto->foilFlg,
                    'cardNo' => $dto->cardNo,
                    'promotionName' => $dto->promotionNameJp,
                    'productId' => $dto->productId,
                    'productNameJp' => $dto->productNameJp,
                    'productNameEn' => $dto->productNameEn,
                    'rarityCode' => $dto->rarityCode,
                    'storageCodeName' => $dto->storageCodeName,
                    'languageClasses' => [],
                ];
            }

            // Language レベル
            if (!isset($cards[$cardId]['details'][$detailId]['languageClasses'][$languageCode])) {
                $cards[$cardId]['details'][$detailId]['languageClasses'][$languageCode] = [
                    'conditionClasses' => [],
                ];
            }

            // ConditionClassレベル
            if (!isset($cards[$cardId]['details'][$detailId]['languageClasses'][$languageCode]['conditionClasses'][$conditionCode])) {
                $cards[$cardId]['details'][$detailId]['languageClasses'][$languageCode]['conditionClasses'][$conditionCode] = [];
            }

            // ProductClass情報を追加
            $cards[$cardId]['details'][$detailId]['languageClasses'][$languageCode]['conditionClasses'][$conditionCode] = [
                'productClassId' => $dto->productClassId,
                'productClassCode' => $dto->productClassCode,
                'buyPrice' => $dto->buyPrice,
                'price' => $dto->standardPrice,
                'stock' => $dto->stock,
                'sectionId' => $dto->sectionId,
            ];
        }

        return ['cards' => empty($cards) ? new \stdClass() : $cards];
    }
}

namespace Eccube\Controller\App\MTGBuyer\V1\Admin;

use Eccube\Controller\AbstractController;
use Eccube\Entity\Member;
use Eccube\Entity\ProductStock;
use Eccube\Exception\App\NotFoundException;
use Eccube\Exception\App\UnauthenticatedException;
use Eccube\Repository\Master\MtbCardRepository;
use Eccube\Service\App\MTGBuyer\V1\BuyingCardsFormatter;
use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\JsonResponse;
use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Request;
use Symfony\Component\Routing\Attribute\Route;
use Symfony\Component\Security\Http\Attribute\IsGranted;

/**
 * 買取用商品情報を取得するコントローラー
 * 現行の仕様と設計書を優先して、"admin"を抜いたパスにしている。
 */
#[IsGranted('IS_AUTHENTICATED_FULLY')]
class BuyingController extends AbstractController
{
    public function __construct(
        private readonly MtbCardRepository $mtbCardRepository,
        private readonly BuyingCardsFormatter $buyingCardsFormatter,
    ) {
    }

    /**
     * カード詳細IDから買取用商品情報を取得
     */
    #[Route('/%eccube_api_v1_route%/buying/{cardDetailId}.json', name: 'api_buying', methods: ['GET'])]
    public function getByCardDetailId(int $cardDetailId): JsonResponse
    {
        $Member = $this->getUser();
        if (!$Member instanceof Member) {
            throw new UnauthenticatedException('認証エラー');
        }

        [$storeId, $stockLocationId] = $this->resolveBuyingStockContext($Member);

        $cards = $this->mtbCardRepository->getBuyingCardsByCardDetailIds([$cardDetailId], $storeId, $stockLocationId);
        if (empty($cards)) {
            throw new NotFoundException('カードが見つかりません');
        }

        return new JsonResponse($this->buyingCardsFormatter->format($cards));
    }

    /**
     * 商品IDのリストから買取用商品情報を取得
     */
    #[Route('/%eccube_api_v1_route%/buying/products', name: 'api_buying_products', methods: ['POST'])]
    public function getByProductIds(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $idsParam = $request->request->get('ids', '');

        // カンマ区切りを配列に変換し、数字のみをフィルタリング
        $productIds = array_filter(
            explode(',', $idsParam),
            static fn (string $id) => preg_match('/^\d+$/', trim($id)) === 1
        );
        $productIds = array_map('intval', $productIds);

        // カード商品以外のIDだけが来た場合、0件となり得るためエラーとはしない
        if (empty($productIds)) {
            return new JsonResponse(['cards' => []]);
        }

        $Member = $this->getUser();
        if (!$Member instanceof Member) {
            throw new UnauthenticatedException('認証エラー');
        }

        [$storeId, $stockLocationId] = $this->resolveBuyingStockContext($Member);

        $cards = $this->mtbCardRepository->getBuyingCardsByProductIds($productIds, $storeId, $stockLocationId);

        return new JsonResponse($this->buyingCardsFormatter->format($cards));
    }

    /**
     * カード名から買取用商品情報を取得
     */
    #[Route('/%eccube_api_v1_route%/search', name: 'api_search', methods: ['POST'])]
    public function getByCardName(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $cardName = $request->request->get('name', '');

        if (empty($cardName)) {
            return new JsonResponse(['cards' => []]);
        }

        $Member = $this->getUser();
        if (!$Member instanceof Member) {
            throw new UnauthenticatedException('認証エラー');
        }

        [$storeId, $stockLocationId] = $this->resolveBuyingStockContext($Member);

        $cards = $this->mtbCardRepository->getBuyingCardsByCardName($cardName, $storeId, $stockLocationId);

        return new JsonResponse($this->buyingCardsFormatter->format($cards));
    }

    /**
     * メンバーの所属店舗IDと在庫場所IDを取得
     *
     * @return array{0: int, 1: int}
     */
    private function resolveBuyingStockContext(Member $Member): array
    {
        return [
            $Member->getBaseInfo()->getId(),
            ProductStock::STOCK_LOCATION_ECCUBE, // ECCUBE在庫固定。スマレジ在庫は扱わない。
        ];
    }
}

    {

    /**
     * 表示フラグがtrueの部門一覧の取得
     */
    #[Route('/%eccube_api_v1_route%/admin/sections.json', name: 'api_admin_sections', methods: ['GET'])]

                'section_id' => $s->getId(),
                'name' => $s->getName(),
                'code' => $s->getCode(),
            ],
            $Sections
        );

        return new JsonResponse($payload);
    }

/*
 * This file is part of EC-CUBE
 *

namespace Eccube\Security\AccessToken;

use Eccube\Exception\App\InvalidTokenException;
use Jose\Component\Core\AlgorithmManager;
use Jose\Component\Core\JWK;
use Jose\Component\KeyManagement\JWKFactory;
use Jose\Component\Signature\Algorithm\HS256;
use Jose\Component\Signature\JWSBuilder;
use Jose\Component\Signature\JWSVerifier;
use Jose\Component\Signature\Serializer\CompactSerializer;
use Jose\Component\Signature\Serializer\JWSSerializerManager;

/**
 * JWTトークンの生成・検証を行うサービス
 */
class JwtTokenService
{
    private JWSSerializerManager $serializerManager;
    private JWSBuilder $jwsBuilder;
    private JWSVerifier $jwsVerifier;
    private ?JWK $secretKey = null;

    public function __construct(
        private readonly string $jwtSecret,
    ) {
        $algorithmManager = new AlgorithmManager([new HS256()]);
        $this->serializerManager = new JWSSerializerManager([new CompactSerializer()]);
        $this->jwsBuilder = new JWSBuilder($algorithmManager);
        $this->jwsVerifier = new JWSVerifier($algorithmManager);

        $this->initializeKey();
    }

    /**
     * シークレットキーを初期化する
     */
    private function initializeKey(): void
    {
        if ($this->jwtSecret === '') {
            return;
        }

        $rawSecret = base64_decode($this->jwtSecret, true);
        if ($rawSecret === false) {
            throw new \RuntimeException('JWT_SECRET must be a valid base64 encoded string.');
        }

        $this->secretKey = JWKFactory::createFromSecret($rawSecret);
    }

    /**
     * JWTトークンを生成する
     *
     * @param int $memberId Member ID
     *
     * @return string JWTトークン
     */
    public function createToken(int $memberId): string
    {
        if ($this->secretKey === null) {
            throw new \RuntimeException('JWT secret is not configured. Please set JWT_SECRET.');
        }

        $payload = json_encode([
            'sub' => (string) $memberId,
        ], JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR);

        $jws = $this->jwsBuilder
            ->create()
            ->withPayload($payload)
            ->addSignature($this->secretKey, ['alg' => 'HS256'])
            ->build();

        return $this->serializerManager->serialize('jws_compact', $jws, 0);
    }

    /**
     * JWTトークンを検証し、ペイロードを返す
     *
     * @param string $token JWTトークン
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed> ペイロード
     *
     * @throws InvalidTokenException トークンが無効な場合
     */
    public function verifyToken(string $token): array
    {
        if ($this->secretKey === null) {
            throw new \RuntimeException('JWT secret is not configured. Please set JWT_SECRET.');
        }

        try {
            $jws = $this->serializerManager->unserialize($token);
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            throw new InvalidTokenException('Invalid token format', $e);
        }

        $isValid = $this->jwsVerifier->verifyWithKey($jws, $this->secretKey, 0);

        if (!$isValid) {
            throw new InvalidTokenException('Token signature verification failed');
        }

        $payload = $jws->getPayload();
        if ($payload === null) {
            throw new InvalidTokenException('Token payload is empty');
        }

        try {
            $decoded = json_decode($payload, true, 512, JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR);
        } catch (\JsonException $e) {
            throw new InvalidTokenException('Invalid token payload', $e);
        }

        return $decoded;
<?php

/*
 * This file is part of EC-CUBE
 *
 * Copyright(c) EC-CUBE CO.,LTD. All Rights Reserved.
 *
 * http://www.ec-cube.co.jp/

 * For the full copyright and license information, please view the LICENSE

namespace Eccube\Security\AccessToken;

use Eccube\Entity\Member;
use Eccube\Exception\App\InvalidTokenException;
use Eccube\Repository\MemberRepository;
use Psr\Log\LoggerInterface;
use Symfony\Component\Security\Core\Exception\BadCredentialsException;
use Symfony\Component\Security\Http\AccessToken\AccessTokenHandlerInterface;
use Symfony\Component\Security\Http\Authenticator\Passport\Badge\UserBadge;

/**
 * JWTトークンを検証し、認証済みユーザーを返すハンドラー
 *
 * Why: クライアント側にリフレッシュトークンの仕組みがないことと
 * トークンに有効期限を設けていないため、DBでMemberの存在を確認する
 */
class JwtTokenHandler implements AccessTokenHandlerInterface
{
    public function __construct(
        private readonly JwtTokenService $jwtTokenService,
        private readonly MemberRepository $memberRepository,
        private readonly LoggerInterface $logger,
    ) {
    }

    #[\Override]
    public function getUserBadgeFrom(string $accessToken): UserBadge
    {
        try {
            $memberId = $this->jwtTokenService->verifyAndGetMemberId($accessToken);
        } catch (InvalidTokenException $e) {
            $this->logger->warning('JWT authentication failed: {message}', [
                'message' => $e->getMessage(),
                'exception' => $e,
            ]);
            throw new BadCredentialsException('Invalid token', 0, $e);
        }

        return new UserBadge(
            (string) $memberId,
            fn (string $identifier): Member => $this->getMember((int) $identifier)
        );
    }

    /**
     * Member IDからMemberを取得する
     *
     * @param int $memberId Member ID
     *
     * @return Member
     *
     * @throws BadCredentialsException Memberが存在しない場合
     */
    private function getMember(int $memberId): Member
    {
        // Why: 旧システムと同じ仕様でworkチェックをしない
        $Member = $this->memberRepository->find($memberId);

        if ($Member === null) {
            $this->logger->warning('JWT authentication failed: Member not found', [
                'member_id' => $memberId,
            ]);
            throw new BadCredentialsException('Member not found');
        }

        return $Member;
    }
}

/*

namespace Eccube\Service\EntityManager;

use Doctrine\Common\Collections\Collection;
use Doctrine\ORM\EntityManagerInterface;
use Eccube\Entity\DtbOtcBuyOrder;
use Eccube\Entity\DtbOtcBuyOrderDetail;
use Eccube\Entity\DtbOtcBuyOrderIndivisualInputProduct;
use Eccube\Entity\DtbOtcBuyOrderStock;
use Eccube\Entity\Master\MtbOtcBuyOrderStatus;
use Eccube\Entity\Member;

class OtcBuyOrderEntityManager
{
    public function __construct(
        private readonly EntityManagerInterface $entityManager,
        private readonly OtcBuyOrderStatusHistoryEntityManager $otcBuyOrderStatusHistoryEntityManager,
    ) {
    }

    /**
     * @param Collection<int, DtbOtcBuyOrderDetail> $OtcBuyOrderDetails
     * @param Collection<int, DtbOtcBuyOrderIndivisualInputProduct> $OtcBuyOrderIndivisualInputProducts
     * @param Collection<int, DtbOtcBuyOrderStock> $OtcBuyOrderStocks
     */
    public function update(DtbOtcBuyOrder $OtcBuyOrder, MtbOtcBuyOrderStatus $OtcBuyOrderStatus, ?bool $qualifiedInvoiceIssuerConfirmationFlg, ?int $smaregiTransactionId, int $totalPrice, Collection $OtcBuyOrderDetails, Collection $OtcBuyOrderIndivisualInputProducts, Collection $OtcBuyOrderStocks, Member $Member, \DateTime $currentTime): void
    {
        $OtcBuyOrder
            ->setOtcBuyOrderStatus($OtcBuyOrderStatus)
            ->setTotalPrice($totalPrice)
            ->setOtcBuyOrderDetails($OtcBuyOrderDetails)
            ->setOtcBuyOrderIndivisualInputProducts($OtcBuyOrderIndivisualInputProducts)
            ->setOtcBuyOrderStocks($OtcBuyOrderStocks)
            ->setMember($Member)
            ->setUpdateDate($currentTime);

        // 買取成立の場合、completeDateとsmaregiTransactionIdを設定
        if ($OtcBuyOrderStatus->isComplete()) {
            $OtcBuyOrder
                ->setCompleteDate($currentTime)
                ->setTransactionId($smaregiTransactionId);

        // 買取キャンセルの場合、cancelDateを設定
        } elseif ($OtcBuyOrderStatus->isCancel()) {
            $OtcBuyOrder->setCancelDate($currentTime);
        }

        if ($qualifiedInvoiceIssuerConfirmationFlg) {
            $OtcBuyOrder->setQualifiedInvoiceIssuerConfirmationFlg($qualifiedInvoiceIssuerConfirmationFlg);
        }
        $this->entityManager->persist($OtcBuyOrder);
    }

    /**
     * @param DtbOtcBuyOrder $OtcBuyOrder
     * @param MtbOtcBuyOrderStatus $OtcBuyOrderStatus
     */
    public function updateStatus(DtbOtcBuyOrder $OtcBuyOrder, MtbOtcBuyOrderStatus $OtcBuyOrderStatus): void
    {
        $OtcBuyOrder->setOtcBuyOrderStatus($OtcBuyOrderStatus);
        $this->entityManager->persist($OtcBuyOrder);
    }

    /**
     * ステータス更新 + 履歴保存を一括で行う
     */
    public function updateStatusWithHistory(
        DtbOtcBuyOrder $OtcBuyOrder,
        MtbOtcBuyOrderStatus $NextStatus,
        Member $Member,
        \DateTime $currentTime,
    ): void {
        $this->updateStatus($OtcBuyOrder, $NextStatus);
        $this->otcBuyOrderStatusHistoryEntityManager->save($OtcBuyOrder, $NextStatus, $Member, $currentTime);
    }
}

    90	業務ロジック
    91	応答に含めない商品規格

    93	商品に商品画像が1件も結び付いていない
    94	カード詳細にレアリティが結び付いていない
    95	商品規格に、買取価格・部門IDを保持する付随情報が結び付いていない
    96	その付随情報に言語が結び付いていない
    97	その付随情報に状態が結び付いていない

    58	A06-07 商品IDリストから買取用商品情報を取得 / API 店頭買取管理 — 商品IDリストから買取用商品情報を取得
    59	業務ロジック
    60	取得対象の決め方
    61	商品画像・カード・カード詳細・レアリティ・言語・カード状態のいずれかが登録されていない商品は、削除済みでなくても結果に含まれない。カードセット・プロモーション・保管コードは未設定でも結果に含まれる。
    62	商品IDリストが未指定・空文字のときも入力エラーとはせず、該当する商品が無いものとして扱う。
    63	同じ組に複数の商品規格が該当したときの扱い
    64	同じカードID・カード詳細ID・言語コード・状態コードの組に複数の商品規格が該当した場合、応答に残るのは1件だけで、後から取得したものが先のものを上書きする。取得順は指定していないため、どれが残るかは定まらない。
    65	値の取り方
    66	取得した値をそのまま応答へ整形して返す。表示用の丸め・税計算・ポイント計算・在庫数量の再計算は行わない。

    60	A06-08 カード名から買取用商品情報を取得 / API 店頭買取管理 — カード名から買取用商品情報を取得
    61	業務ロジック
    62	検索条件
    63	カード名に含まれる % と _ はエスケープし、ワイルドカードではなく文字そのものとして扱う。
    64	応答に含めない商品規格
    65	商品または商品規格に次のいずれかが結び付いていない場合、その商品規格は応答に含めない。
    66	商品に結び付く商品画像
    67	商品に結び付く付随情報と、そこにぶら下がるカード詳細・カード・レアリティ
    68	買取価格・部門IDを保持する商品規格の付随情報と、そこにぶら下がる言語・状態
    69	同じ区分に複数の商品規格が該当したときの扱い
    70	応答はカード・カード詳細・言語・状態の組でひとつの商品規格を保持する。同じ組に複数の商品規格が該当した場合、応答に残るのは最後に取得した1件だけで、それ以外は応答から失われる。
    71	取得結果の並べ方
    72	検索で得たカードだけを1始まりの連番でキー付けし直して返す。各カードの配下は、カード詳細ID・言語コード・状態コードの順で入れ子にする。ページングの件数（総件数・1ページあたり件数など）は返さない。
    73	値の取り方
    74	買取価格・販売価格・在庫数は、永続化済みの値をそのまま返す。応答を組み立てるときの丸め・補正・再計算は行わない。
    75	入出力
    76	種類	内容
    77	成功時出力	最上位に cards を置き、その配下にカードごとの情報を入れて返す。
    78	出力: 応答フィールド
    79	フィールド	型	内容
    80	cards	object	1始まりの連番をキーとするオブジェクト。値は各カードの情報
    81	cards.<n>.cardNameJp	string	カードの日本語名
    82	cards.<n>.cardNameEn	string	カードの英語名
    83	cards.<n>.imageFileName	string	カード画像のファイル名
    84	cards.<n>.details	object	カード詳細IDをキーとするオブジェクト。値は各カード詳細の情報
    85	cardsetCode	string	カードセットのコード。カードセット未設定のときはnull
--
    93	rarityCode	string	レアリティのコード
    94	storageCodeName	string	保管コードの名称。未設定のときはnull
    95	languageClasses	object	言語コードをキーとするオブジェクト
    96	conditionClasses	object	状態コードをキーとするオブジェクト。値は商品規格単位の情報
    97	productClassId	integer	商品規格ID
    98	productCode	string	商品コード
    99	buyPrice	integer	買取価格。未設定のときはnull
   100	price	string	販売価格

    57	現行仕様
    58	A06-13 本人確認更新 / API 店頭買取管理 — 本人確認更新
    59	業務ロジック
    60	認証
    61	認証用トークンをヘッダ jwt-token で受け取り、署名方式 HS256 で検証したうえで、トークンが指す管理者会員を特定する。ヘッダが無いとき、署名が不正なとき、トークンが指す管理者会員が存在しないときは、いずれも認証拒否（HTTP 401）とし、更新しない。
    62	判定の順序
    63	認証、店頭買取受注の取得、証明書IDの妥当性の順に判定し、成立しないものが出た時点で処理を打ち切り、以降の判定は行わない。認証できないときは受注IDと証明書IDの誤りは判定されず、受注が存在しないときは証明書IDの誤りは判定されない。
    64	証明書IDを送っていないときは、マスタに存在しない値を送ったときと同じ扱いになる。
    65	更新する項目
    66	証明書のほかに、更新担当者へ認証で特定した管理者会員を、更新日時へ更新時点の日時を設定する。
    67	入出力
    68	入力: 認証情報
    69	パラメータ	位置	型	必須／任意	説明
    70	jwt-token	ヘッダ	string	必須	認証用のトークン
    71	出力: 認証できないとき
    72	HTTPステータス	条件
    73	401	認証用トークンの欠落・署名不正・トークンが指す管理者会員なしのとき
    74	入出力: エンドポイントの別名
    75	拡張子 .json を付けないパス（/api/admin/otcBuyOrder/{id}/identification）でも、同じメソッドで同じ更新を受け付ける。
    76	入出力: 永続化
    77	操作	契機
    78	更新	本人確認更新（本人確認証明書・更新担当者・更新日時）
    79	リニューアル後の仕様
    80	表示メッセージ
    81	メッセージID	表示位置	画面上の文言	表示条件	後続処理
    82	A06-13-MSG-001	API応答JSON（errors配列）	買取情報が見つかりません	本人確認更新で、パスの受注IDに対応する店頭買取受注が存在しないとき	HTTP 404・{code, errors}を返し、更新しない
    83	A06-13-MSG-002	API応答JSON（errors配列）	正しい証明書IDを入力してください	本人確認更新で本人確認証明書のIDが未指定のとき	HTTP 400・{code, errors}を返し、更新しない
    84	A06-13-MSG-003	API応答JSON（errors配列）	正しい証明書IDを入力してください	本人確認更新で指定した本人確認証明書のIDがマスタに存在しないとき	HTTP 404・{code, errors}を返し、更新しない
    85	A06-13-MSG-004	API応答JSON（errors配列）	認証エラー	本人確認更新で認証済み利用者を管理者会員として取得できないとき	HTTP 401・{code, errors}を返し、更新しない

    16	namespace Eccube\Exception\App;
    17	
    18	use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response;
    19	
    20	abstract class BaseApiException extends \RuntimeException
    21	{
    22	    /**
    23	     * @param array<int, string> $errors
    24	     */
    25	    public function __construct(
    26	        protected array $errors,
    27	        protected int $statusCode = Response::HTTP_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
    28	        protected string $logLevel = 'error',
    29	        ?\Throwable $previous = null,
    30	    ) {
    31	        $message = !empty($errors) ? implode(', ', $errors) : 'API error';
    32	        parent::__construct($message, $statusCode, $previous);
    33	    }
    34	
    35	    public function getStatusCode(): int
    36	    {
    37	        return $this->statusCode;
    38	    }
    39	
    40	    public function getLogLevel(): string
    41	    {
    42	        return $this->logLevel;
    43	    }
    44	
    45	    /**
    46	     * @return array<int, string>
    47	     */
    48	    public function getErrors(): array
    49	    {
    50	        return $this->errors;
    51	    }
    52	}

    16	namespace Eccube\Exception\App;
    17	
    18	use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response;
    19	
    20	class NotFoundException extends BaseApiException
    21	{
    22	    /**
    23	     * @param string $message エラーメッセージ
    24	     * @param ?\Throwable $previous
    25	     * @param string $logLevel ログレベル
    26	     */
    27	    public function __construct(string $message = 'リソースが見つかりません', ?\Throwable $previous = null, string $logLevel = 'info')
    28	    {
    29	        parent::__construct(
    30	            errors: [$message],
    31	            statusCode: Response::HTTP_NOT_FOUND,
    32	            logLevel: $logLevel,
    33	            previous: $previous
    34	        );
    35	    }
    36	}
    70	     *
    71	     * @return string JWTトークン
    72	     */
    73	    public function createToken(int $memberId): string
    74	    {
    75	        if ($this->secretKey === null) {
    76	            throw new \RuntimeException('JWT secret is not configured. Please set JWT_SECRET.');
    77	        }
    78	
    79	        $payload = json_encode([
    80	            'sub' => (string) $memberId,
    81	        ], JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR);
    82	
    83	        $jws = $this->jwsBuilder
    84	            ->create()
    85	            ->withPayload($payload)
    86	            ->addSignature($this->secretKey, ['alg' => 'HS256'])
    87	            ->build();
    88	
    89	        return $this->serializerManager->serialize('jws_compact', $jws, 0);
    90	    }
    91	
    92	    /**
    93	     * JWTトークンを検証し、ペイロードを返す
    94	     *
    95	     * @param string $token JWTトークン
    96	     *
    97	     * @return array<string, mixed> ペイロード
    98	     *
    99	     * @throws InvalidTokenException トークンが無効な場合
   100	     */
   101	    public function verifyToken(string $token): array
   102	    {
   103	        if ($this->secretKey === null) {
   104	            throw new \RuntimeException('JWT secret is not configured. Please set JWT_SECRET.');
   105	        }
   106	
   107	        try {
   108	            $jws = $this->serializerManager->unserialize($token);
   109	        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
   110	            throw new InvalidTokenException('Invalid token format', $e);
   111	        }
   112	
   113	        $isValid = $this->jwsVerifier->verifyWithKey($jws, $this->secretKey, 0);
   114	
   115	        if (!$isValid) {
   116	            throw new InvalidTokenException('Token signature verification failed');
   117	        }
   118	
   119	        $payload = $jws->getPayload();
   120	        if ($payload === null) {
   121	            throw new InvalidTokenException('Token payload is empty');
   122	        }
   123	
   124	        try {
   125	            $decoded = json_decode($payload, true, 512, JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR);
   126	        } catch (\JsonException $e) {
   127	            throw new InvalidTokenException('Invalid token payload', $e);
   128	        }
   129	
   130	        return $decoded;
   131	    }
   132	
   133	    /**
   134	     * トークンを検証し、Member IDを取得する
   135	     *
   136	     * @param string $token JWT文字列
   137	     *
   138	     * @return int Member ID
   139	     *
   140	     * @throws InvalidTokenException トークンが無効な場合
   141	     */
   142	    public function verifyAndGetMemberId(string $token): int
   143	    {
   144	        $payload = $this->verifyToken($token);
   145	
   146	        if (!isset($payload['sub'])) {
   147	            throw new InvalidTokenException('Token payload missing required claim: sub');
   148	        }
   149	
   150	        $memberId = filter_var($payload['sub'], FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
   151	        if ($memberId === false) {
   152	            throw new InvalidTokenException('Invalid member ID in token');
   153	        }
   154	
   155	        return $memberId;
   156	    }
   157	}

    59	        foreach ($dtoData as $dto) {
    60	            if ($dto->languageCode === null) {
    61	                continue;
    62	            }
    63	
    64	            if ($dto->conditionCode === null) {
    65	                continue;
    66	            }
    67	
    68	            $cardId = $dto->cardId;
    69	            $detailId = $dto->detailId;
    70	            $languageCode = $dto->languageCode;
    71	            $conditionCode = $dto->conditionCode;
    72	
    73	            // Cardレベル
    74	            if (!isset($cards[$cardId])) {
    75	                $cards[$cardId] = [
    76	                    'cardNameJp' => $dto->cardNameJp,
    77	                    'cardNameEn' => $dto->cardNameEn,
    78	                    'imageFileName' => $dto->imageFileName,
    79	                    'details' => [],
    80	                ];
    81	            }
    82	
    83	            // Detailレベル
    84	            if (!isset($cards[$cardId]['details'][$detailId])) {
    85	                $cards[$cardId]['details'][$detailId] = [
    86	                    'cardsetCode' => $dto->cardSetCode,
    87	                    'cardsetName' => $dto->cardSetNameJp,
    88	                    'foilFlg' => $dto->foilFlg,
    89	                    'cardNo' => $dto->cardNo,
    90	                    'promotionName' => $dto->promotionNameJp,
    91	                    'productId' => $dto->productId,
    92	                    'productNameJp' => $dto->productNameJp,
    93	                    'productNameEn' => $dto->productNameEn,
    94	                    'rarityCode' => $dto->rarityCode,
    95	                    'storageCodeName' => $dto->storageCodeName,
    96	                    'languageClasses' => [],
    97	                ];
    98	            }
    99	
   100	            // Language レベル
   101	            if (!isset($cards[$cardId]['details'][$detailId]['languageClasses'][$languageCode])) {
   102	                $cards[$cardId]['details'][$detailId]['languageClasses'][$languageCode] = [
   103	                    'conditionClasses' => [],
   104	                ];
   105	            }
   106	
   107	            // ConditionClassレベル
   108	            if (!isset($cards[$cardId]['details'][$detailId]['languageClasses'][$languageCode]['conditionClasses'][$conditionCode])) {
   109	                $cards[$cardId]['details'][$detailId]['languageClasses'][$languageCode]['conditionClasses'][$conditionCode] = [];
   110	            }
   111	
   112	            // ProductClass情報を追加
   113	            $cards[$cardId]['details'][$detailId]['languageClasses'][$languageCode]['conditionClasses'][$conditionCode] = [
   114	                'productClassId' => $dto->productClassId,
   115	                'productClassCode' => $dto->productClassCode,
   116	                'buyPrice' => $dto->buyPrice,
   117	                'price' => $dto->standardPrice,
   118	                'stock' => $dto->stock,
   119	                'sectionId' => $dto->sectionId,
   120	            ];
   121	        }
   122	
   123	        return ['cards' => empty($cards) ? new \stdClass() : $cards];
   124	    }
   125	}

   144	    /**
   145	     * 買取カード情報取得用の共通クエリビルダー
   146	     * Product, ProductClassが公開・非公開（廃止以外）のもののみを取得する
   147	     * 高額カードは除外する
   148	     *
   149	     * @param int $storeId 店舗ID
   150	     * @param int $stockLocationId 在庫場所ID
   151	     */
   152	    private function buildBuyingCardsQuery(int $storeId, int $stockLocationId): QueryBuilder
   153	    {
   154	        return $this->createQueryBuilder('card')
   155	            ->join('card.CardDetails', 'cardDetail')
   156	            ->join('cardDetail.Products', 'product')
   157	            ->join('product.ProductClasses', 'productClass')
   158	            ->join('productClass.ProductStock', 'productStock')  // ProductClassからProductStockを取得
   159	            ->leftJoin('product.ProductImage', 'productImage', 'WITH', 'productImage.sort_no = 0')  // 最初の商品画像
   160	            ->join('productClass.CardCondition', 'cardCondition')
   161	            ->leftJoin('product.StorageCode', 'storageCode')
   162	            ->leftJoin('cardDetail.Cardset', 'cardset')
   163	            ->leftJoin('cardDetail.Promotion', 'promotion')
   164	            ->join('productClass.Language', 'language')
   165	            ->join('cardDetail.Rarity', 'rarity')
   166	            ->leftJoin('productClass.Section', 'section')
   167	            ->andWhere('IDENTITY(product.Status) IN (:productStatusIds)')
   168	            ->andWhere('IDENTITY(productClass.Status) IN (:productStatusIds)')
   169	            ->andWhere('productStock.baseInfo = :storeId')
   170	            ->andWhere('productStock.stockLocationId = :stockLocationId')
   171	            ->andWhere('productClass.high_price_code IS NULL') // 高額カード除外
   172	            ->setParameter('productStatusIds', [ProductStatus::DISPLAY_SHOW, ProductStatus::DISPLAY_HIDE]) // 移植元の論理削除＝廃止とみなして、公開・非公開を抽出
   173	            ->setParameter('storeId', $storeId)
   174	            ->setParameter('stockLocationId', $stockLocationId)
   175	            ->select('
   176	                card.id AS cardId,
   177	                card.name_jp AS cardNameJp,
   178	                card.name_en AS cardNameEn,
   179	                cardDetail.id AS detailId,
   180	                cardset.code AS cardSetCode,
   181	                cardset.name_jp AS cardSetNameJp,
   182	                cardDetail.foil_flg AS foilFlg,
   183	                cardDetail.card_no AS cardNo,
   184	                promotion.name_jp AS promotionNameJp,
   185	                product.id AS productId,
   186	                product.name AS productNameJp,
   187	                product.name_en AS productNameEn,
   188	                rarity.code AS rarityCode,
   189	                storageCode.name AS storageCodeName,
   190	                language.code AS languageCode,
   191	                cardCondition.code AS conditionCode,
   192	                productClass.id AS productClassId,
   193	                productClass.code AS productClassCode,
   194	                productClass.buy_price AS buyPrice,
   195	                productClass.standardPrice AS standardPrice,
   196	                productStock.stock AS stock,
   197	                section.id AS sectionId,
   198	                productImage.file_name AS imageFileName
   199	            ');
   200	    }

   231	    #[Route('/%eccube_api_v1_route%/admin/otcBuyOrder/{id}/doubleCheckMember.json', name: 'api_admin_otc_buy_order_update_double_check_member', methods: ['PUT'])]
   232	    public function updateDoubleCheckMember(int $id, Request $request): JsonResponse
   233	    {
   234	        $OtcBuyOrder = $this->dtbOtcBuyOrderRepository->find($id);
   235	        if (!$OtcBuyOrder instanceof DtbOtcBuyOrder) {
   236	            throw new NotFoundException('買取情報が見つかりません');
   237	        }
   238	
   239	        $raw = $request->request->get('double_check_member_id');
   240	        if ($raw === null) {
   241	            throw new MissingRequiredParameterException('ダブルチェック者を入力してください');
   242	        }
   243	
   244	        $Member = $this->getUser();
   245	        if (!$Member instanceof Member) {
   246	            throw new UnauthenticatedException('認証エラー');
   247	        }
   248	
   249	        $doubleCheckMemberId = (int) $raw;
   250	
   251	        $DoubleCheckMember = $this->memberRepository->find($doubleCheckMemberId);
   252	        if (!$DoubleCheckMember instanceof Member) {
   253	            throw new NotFoundException('ダブルチェック者が見つかりません');
   254	        }
   255	
   256	        try {
   257	            $this->updateDoubleCheckMemberAction->handle(new UpdateDoubleCheckMemberInput($OtcBuyOrder, $Member, $DoubleCheckMember));
   258	
   259	            // カスタムエラーの場合はそのまま返す
   260	        } catch (BaseApiException $e) {
   261	            throw $e;
   262	            // システムエラーの場合は隠蔽して500エラーを返す
   263	        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
   264	            throw new InternalException('システムエラーが発生しました', $e);
   265	        }
   266	
   267	        return new JsonResponse(['code' => Response::HTTP_OK], Response::HTTP_OK);
   268	    }

codex
中間結論として、共通クエリの画像 `leftJoin` を原因とする2件は、別API名でも同一修正箇所・同一欠陥である可能性が高いです。また本人確認更新は、正本後段の「リニューアル後の仕様」が404を明記し、実装も404なので、優先規約に照らして指摘維持が難しい状態です。残りは別経路による充足や重要度を最終確認しています。
